(4.15b)

\displaystyle J_{E}{}^{a}={}

\displaystyle\frac{{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.76923pt}{\resizebox{481531}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle+$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.76923pt}{\resizebox{481531}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle+$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.57141pt}{\resizebox{516533}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle+$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.57141pt}{\resizebox{583338}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}}}}}^{2}{{\sigma}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{392747}{262143}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle-$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{392747}{262143}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle-$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{429219}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle-$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{519722}{262143}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}}}}}}^{a}-{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{392747}{262143}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle-$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{392747}{262143}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle-$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{429219}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle-$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{519722}{262143}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}}}}}}^{2}{{\sigma}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.76923pt}{\resizebox{481531}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle+$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.76923pt}{\resizebox{481531}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle+$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.57141pt}{\resizebox{516533}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle+$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.57141pt}{\resizebox{583338}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}}}}}^{a}}{2\lambda^{2}}=\frac{-(\lambda^{2}+\omega^{2})\omega^{a}+2\lambda\omega\nabla^{a}\lambda}{\lambda^{2}}.

(4.15c)

With the assumptions and definitions above, the currents
J_{T}{}^{a},J_{D}{}^{a},J_{E}{}^{a}
, called Translation, Dilation and Ehlers currents, respectively, are conserved, that is
\nabla_{a}J_{T}{}^{a}=\nabla_{a}J_{D}{}^{a}=\nabla_{a}J_{E}{}^{a}=0
. Hence, integrating the currents over closed 3-surfaces enclosing but not intersecting nuts and bolts defines quasilocally conserved charges.

Next, we will compute the charges corresponding to
J_{T}{}^{a}
.

Definition 4.4
.

Let
K
be a nut or a bolt in
\mathcal{M}
. Let
U
be an open set containing
K
with smooth boundary
S
, so that neither
U
nor
S
intersects any other nut or bolt. Let
n^{a}
be the inward pointing unit normal to
S
. The charge of
K
is

\displaystyle N(K)=\frac{1}{8\pi}\int_{S}J^{T}_{a}n^{a}\,d\mu=-\frac{1}{8\pi}\int_{S}\lambda^{-2}\omega_{a}n^{a}\,d\mu.

(4.16)

We now discuss the geometry of bolts in more detail. For a bolt
B
we denote the volume form of its tangent bundle by
\epsilon^{\perp}{}_{ab}
and the volume form of its normal bundle by
\epsilon^{\parallel}{}_{ab}
. They are both two-forms on
T\mathcal{M}
defined at points in
B
, and they are related by

\displaystyle\epsilon_{abcd}\epsilon^{\perp}{}^{cd}=2\epsilon^{\parallel}{}_{ab},\qquad\epsilon_{abcd}\epsilon^{\parallel}{}^{cd}=2\epsilon^{\perp}{}_{ab}.

(4.17)

On
B
we have

\displaystyle F_{ab}=\nabla_{a}\xi_{b}=\kappa\epsilon^{\perp}{}_{ab}

(4.18)

where
\kappa
is constant, and

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{ab}=\kappa(\epsilon^{\perp}{}_{ab}\pm\epsilon^{\parallel}{}_{ab}).

(4.19)

Define the curvature scalars

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{\parallel\parallel}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}R_{abcd}\epsilon^{\parallel}{}^{ab}\epsilon^{\parallel}{}^{cd},

(4.20)

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{\parallel\perp}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}R_{abcd}\epsilon^{\parallel}{}^{ab}\epsilon^{\perp}{}^{cd},

(4.21)

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{\perp\perp}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}R_{abcd}\epsilon^{\perp}{}^{ab}\epsilon^{\perp}{}^{cd}.

(4.22)

Since
B
is totally geodesic we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{\parallel\parallel}=R^{B}=2\mathcal{K}^{B},

(4.23)

and since
\mathcal{M}
is Ricci-flat,

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{\perp\perp}=R^{B}=2\mathcal{K}^{B}.

(4.24)

Proposition 4.5
.

For a nut
P
with surface gravities
\kappa^{1},\kappa^{2}
we have

\displaystyle N(P)=\frac{\pi}{2\kappa^{1}\kappa^{2}}.

(4.25)

For a bolt
B
with surface gravity
\kappa
we have

\displaystyle N(B)={}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{8\kappa^{2}}\int_{B}\mathcal{R}^{\parallel\perp}d\mu

\displaystyle={}

\displaystyle-\frac{\pi}{2\kappa^{2}}B\!\cdot\!B.

(4.26)

Proof.

We will compute the integral (
4.16
) when
S=S(\rho)
is a connected component of the level set
\{\lambda=\rho\}
. Then
n^{a}=-\frac{\nabla^{a}\lambda}{|\nabla^{a}\lambda|}
. Since
\lambda^{-2}\omega_{a}
is divergence free we have

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle=\nabla^{a}(\lambda^{-2}\omega_{a})

\displaystyle=-2\lambda^{-3}(\nabla^{a}\lambda)\omega_{a}+\lambda^{-2}\nabla^{a}\omega_{a}

\displaystyle=2\lambda^{-3}|\nabla\lambda|n^{a}\omega_{a}+\lambda^{-2}\nabla^{a}\omega_{a},

(4.27)

so

\displaystyle\lambda^{-2}n^{a}\omega_{a}=-\frac{1}{2\lambda|\nabla\lambda|}\nabla^{a}\omega_{a}=-\frac{1}{|\nabla\lambda^{2}|}\nabla^{a}\omega_{a}.

(4.28)

Here,

\displaystyle\nabla^{a}\omega_{a}

\displaystyle=\nabla^{a}(\epsilon_{abcd}\xi^{b}(\nabla^{c}\xi^{d}))

\displaystyle=\epsilon_{abcd}(\nabla^{a}\xi^{b})(\nabla^{c}\xi^{d})+\epsilon_{abcd}\xi^{b}(\nabla^{a}\nabla^{c}\xi^{d})

\displaystyle=4\nu+\epsilon_{abcd}\xi^{b}R^{dca}{}_{f}\xi^{f}

\displaystyle=4\nu

(4.29)

where the second term vanishes by the Bianchi identity.

Let
\alpha\in\mathbb{R}
, and let
A
,
B
be two expressions which satisfy
A=O(\lambda^{\alpha})
,
B=O(\lambda^{\alpha})
near a component of the fixed point set. We write
A\simeq B
if
A-B=O(\lambda^{\alpha+\delta})
for some
\delta>0
.

Near a nut
P
we have

\displaystyle\nu\simeq 2\kappa^{1}\kappa^{2}

(4.30)

and in normal coordinates,

\displaystyle\lambda\simeq(\kappa^{1})^{2}\left((y^{1})^{2}+(y^{2})^{2}\right)+(\kappa^{2})^{2}\left((y^{3})^{2}+(y^{4})^{2}\right).

(4.31)

Let
D(\delta)
be the connected set containing
P
where
\lambda\leq\delta
. For
\delta
small, the coarea formula applied to the sets
S(\rho^{1/2})
where
\lambda^{2}=\rho
tells us that

\displaystyle\delta\cdot N(P)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\delta}N(P)\,d\rho

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{8\pi}\int_{0}^{\delta}\int_{S(\rho^{1/2})}\lambda^{-2}\omega_{a}n^{a}\,d\mu\,d\rho

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{8\pi}\int_{0}^{\delta}\int_{S(\rho^{1/2})}\frac{4\nu}{|\nabla\lambda^{2}|}\,d\mu\,d\rho

\displaystyle\simeq\frac{8\kappa^{1}\kappa^{2}}{8\pi}\int_{0}^{\delta}\int_{S(\rho^{1/2})}\frac{1}{|\nabla\lambda^{2}|}\,d\mu\,d\rho

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa^{1}\kappa^{2}}{\pi}\int_{D(\delta^{1/2})}1\,d\mu

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa^{1}\kappa^{2}}{\pi}\operatorname{Vol}(D(\delta^{1/2})).

(4.32)

With a linear change of coordinates
(x^{1},x^{2},x^{3},x^{4})=(\kappa^{1}y^{1},\kappa^{1}y^{2},\kappa^{2}y^{3},\kappa^{2}y^{4})
with determinant
(\kappa^{1}\kappa^{2})^{2}
we have that
\lambda\leq\delta^{1/2}
corresponds to
|x|\leq\delta^{1/4}
and we find

\displaystyle\operatorname{Vol}(D(\delta^{1/2}))=\frac{1}{(\kappa^{1}\kappa^{2})^{2}}\cdot\frac{\pi^{2}(\delta^{1/4})^{4}}{2}=\frac{\delta\pi^{2}}{2(\kappa^{1}\kappa^{2})^{2}}.

(4.33)

Together we have

\displaystyle N(P)=\frac{\pi}{2\kappa^{1}\kappa^{2}}.

(4.34)

Next, working near a bolt
B
, introduce local coordinates
x^{a}
on
\mathcal{B}
and coordinates
y^{a}
in the normal direction, with
|y(p)|\cong d(p,\mathcal{B})
. Using the identity

\displaystyle\epsilon^{\perp}{}_{h}{}^{[f}y^{g]}y^{h}=\frac{1}{2}|y|^{2}\epsilon^{\perp fg},

(4.35)

we compute the Taylor expansion of
\nabla^{a}\xi^{b}
to second order in the normal directions to
B
,

\displaystyle\nabla^{a}\xi^{b}

\displaystyle\simeq(\nabla^{a}\xi^{b})|_{B}+(\nabla_{g}\nabla^{a}\xi^{b})|_{B}y^{g}+\frac{1}{2}(\nabla_{h}\nabla_{g}\nabla^{a}\xi^{b})|_{B}y^{g}y^{h}

\displaystyle=\kappa\epsilon^{\perp ab}+(R^{ba}{}_{gf}\xi^{f})|_{B}y^{g}+\frac{1}{2}(\nabla_{h}(R^{ba}{}_{gf}\xi^{f}))|_{B}y^{g}y^{h}

\displaystyle=\kappa\epsilon^{\perp ab}+\frac{1}{2}R^{ba}{}_{gf}(\nabla_{h}\xi^{f})|_{B}y^{g}y^{h}

\displaystyle=\kappa\epsilon^{\perp ab}+\frac{1}{2}\kappa R^{ba}{}_{gf}\epsilon^{\perp}{}_{h}{}^{f}y^{g}y^{h}

\displaystyle=\kappa\left(\epsilon^{\perp ab}+\frac{1}{4}|y|^{2}R^{ba}{}_{gf}\epsilon^{\perp fg}\right).

(4.36)

We find that

\displaystyle 4\nu

\displaystyle\simeq\kappa^{2}\epsilon_{abcd}\left(\epsilon^{\perp ab}+\frac{1}{4}|y|^{2}R^{ba}{}_{gf}\epsilon^{\perp fg}\right)\left(\epsilon^{\perp cd}+\frac{1}{4}|y|^{2}R^{dc}{}_{hi}\epsilon^{\perp ih}\right)

\displaystyle\simeq\kappa^{2}\epsilon_{abcd}\epsilon^{\perp ab}\epsilon^{\perp cd}+\frac{1}{2}\kappa^{2}|y|^{2}\epsilon_{abcd}\epsilon^{\perp ab}R^{dc}{}_{hi}\epsilon^{\perp ih}

\displaystyle=0+\kappa^{2}|y|^{2}\epsilon^{\parallel}{}_{cd}R^{dc}{}_{hi}\epsilon^{\perp ih}

\displaystyle=-2\kappa^{2}|y|^{2}\mathcal{R}^{\parallel\perp}.

(4.37)

Near the bolt
B
we further have

\displaystyle\lambda\simeq\kappa^{2}|y|^{2},\qquad\nabla^{a}\lambda\simeq 2\kappa^{2}y^{a},\qquad|\nabla^{a}\lambda|\simeq 2\kappa^{2}|y|,

(4.38)

so

\displaystyle\lambda^{-2}n^{a}\omega_{a}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2\lambda|\nabla^{a}\lambda|}4\nu

\displaystyle\simeq\frac{1}{2\kappa^{2}|y|^{2}\cdot 2\kappa^{2}|y|}2\kappa^{2}|y|^{2}\mathcal{R}^{\parallel\perp}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\kappa^{2}|y|}\mathcal{R}^{\parallel\perp}.

(4.39)

On the surface
S
around
B
we have
\epsilon=\lambda\simeq\kappa^{2}|y|^{2}
, so the surface
S
consists of circles of circumference
\simeq 2\pi|y|\simeq 2\pi|\kappa|^{-1}\epsilon^{1/2}
normal to
B
. We thus find that

\displaystyle N(B)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{8\pi}\int_{S}\lambda^{-2}\omega_{a}n^{a}\,d\mu

\displaystyle\simeq-\frac{1}{8\pi}\int_{S}\frac{1}{2\kappa^{2}|y|}\mathcal{R}^{\parallel\perp}\,d\mu

\displaystyle\simeq-\frac{1}{8\pi}\int_{B}2\pi|\kappa|^{-1}\epsilon^{1/2}\frac{1}{2|\kappa|\epsilon^{1/2}}\mathcal{R}^{\parallel\perp}\,d\mu

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{8\kappa^{2}}\int_{B}\mathcal{R}^{\parallel\perp}\,d\mu.

(4.40)

Next,
\mathcal{R}^{\parallel\perp}d\mu=4\pi e(NB)
, where
e(NB)
is the Euler class of the normal bundle
NB
of
B
in
\mathcal{M}
. From
[
10
, Theorem 11.17]
we have

\displaystyle\int_{B}\mathcal{R}^{\parallel\perp}\,d\mu=4\pi\int_{B}e(NB)=4\pi B\!\cdot\!B,

(4.41)

which gives the result.
∎

5.
Divergence identities

In this section we derive a family of divergence identities, see (
5.22
) below, depending on a parameter
\beta
taking real values. An alternative derivation on the orbit space, following
[
53
,
54
]
, will be given in Appendix
C
. Equation (
5.22
) with
\beta=1
is the key ingredient of the uniqueness arguments presented in Section
7
.

After a suitable normalization of
\xi
, we may without loss of generality assume that the supremum of
\lambda
is
1
.

Lemma 5.1
.

Assume that the supremum of
\lambda
is
1
. The following holds.

(1)

\displaystyle\lambda={}

\displaystyle 1+O^{*}(r^{-1})

(5.1a)

The twist potential
\omega
can be normalized so that
\omega\rightarrow 0
at
\infty
and in this case,

\displaystyle\omega={}

\displaystyle O^{*}(r^{-1})

(5.1b)

(2)

There are numbers
{{b}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}\leq 0
and
\delta>0
so that

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}=1+{{b}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}r^{-1}+O^{*}(r^{-1-\delta})

(5.2)

If
{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}
is non-constant, then
{{b}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}<0
.

(3)

{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}\equiv 1
or
-1<{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}<1

Proof.

Since
g_{ab}
is Ricci-flat we have
\Delta\xi=0
, and hence

\displaystyle\Delta|\xi|^{2}=2\langle\nabla\xi,\nabla\xi\rangle.

(5.3)

By the maximum principle, we have
|\xi|<1
. By Proposition
B.2
it follows that
|\xi|\to 1
and hence also
\lambda\to 1
at infinity.
We also have by Proposition
B.2
that

\displaystyle\nabla_{a}\xi_{b}=O^{*}(r^{-2})

(5.4)

By Lemma
B.1
we may normalize
\omega
to have
\omega\rightarrow 0
at
\infty
.
Equations (
5.1
) now follow from the definitions of
\lambda
and
\omega
, Equation (
5.4
), and Lemma
B.1
.
From the definition of
{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}
and (
5.4
), we have that
{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}=1+O^{*}(r^{-1})
, and hence, by (
4.7
), (
4.6f
), and (
5.1a
), we have

\displaystyle\Delta{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}={{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2},\quad\text{where ${{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}=O^{*}(r^{-4})$.}

(5.5)

By Proposition
B.4
, it follows that
{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}
has an expansion of the form (
5.2
). For
R
large, define the set
U_{R}=\{r\leq R\}
with boundary

\partial U_{r}=S_{R}=\{r=R\}
. From Gauss’s theorem we get

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{M}}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}d\mu

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{M}}\Delta{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}d\mu

\displaystyle=\lim_{R\to\infty}\int_{U_{R}}\Delta{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}d\mu

\displaystyle=\lim_{R\to\infty}\int_{S_{R}}n^{a}\nabla_{a}{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}d\mu

\displaystyle=\lim_{R\to\infty}\int_{S_{R}}\left(-{{b}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}r^{-2}+O^{*}(r^{-2-\delta})\right)d\mu

\displaystyle=-C{{b}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}},

(5.6)

where
n^{a}
is the outward pointing normal to
S_{r}
. Further,
C>0
is a constant related to the volume of
L
in Definition
2.1
, see similar computations in the proof of Lemma
6.12
. It follows that
{{b}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}<0
unless

{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}\equiv 0
in which case
{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}\equiv 1
. This proves point (
2
). If
{{b}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}<0
, the maximum principle implies that
{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}<1
. We thus get
1>{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}=-{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mp$}}}}}}}+2\lambda\geq-{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mp$}}}}}}}>-1
. This proves point (
3
).
∎

With the normalization used in Lemma
5.1
, we have

\displaystyle\lambda\to{}

\displaystyle 1\quad\text{at $\infty$},

(5.7a)

\displaystyle\omega\to{}

\displaystyle 0\quad\text{at $\infty$},

(5.7b)

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}\to{}

\displaystyle 1\quad\text{at $\infty$}.

(5.7c)

These condition will be imposed throughout the rest of the paper.

We also note the following equivalent conditions for
(\mathcal{M},g_{ab})
to be half-flat, i.e.
{{\mathcal{W}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.76923pt}{\resizebox{481531}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle+$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.76923pt}{\resizebox{481531}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle+$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.57141pt}{\resizebox{516533}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle+$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.57141pt}{\resizebox{583338}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}}}}}_{abcd}\equiv 0
or
{{\mathcal{W}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{392747}{262143}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle-$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{392747}{262143}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle-$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{429219}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle-$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{519722}{262143}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}}}}}}_{abcd}\equiv 0
.

Lemma 5.2
.

For any sign
+
or
-
, the following are equivalent.

(1)

(\mathcal{M},g_{ab})
is half-flat, that is
{{\mathcal{W}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{abcd}\equiv 0
,

(2)

{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}\equiv 0
,

(3)

{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}\equiv 1
.

Proof.

If
{{\mathcal{W}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{abcd}\equiv 0
it follows from (
4.6d
) that
\nabla_{c}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}\equiv 0
, that is
{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}
is constant. By Proposition
B.2
we have that
{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}\to 0
at infinity, and hence
{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}\equiv 0
.
On the other hand, if
{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}\equiv 0
we have
{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{ab}\equiv 0
and from (
4.6g
) it follows that
{{\mathcal{W}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{abcd}\equiv 0
. This shows (
1
)
\Leftrightarrow
(
2
).
From (
4.6b
) it follows that
{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}\equiv 0
if and only if
{{\sigma}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{a}\equiv 0
, which by (
4.7
) is equivalent to
\nabla_{a}{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}\equiv 0
. Since
{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}\to 1
at
\infty
by (
5.7c
) , this means that
{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}\equiv 1
. Hence (
2
)
\Leftrightarrow
(
3
).
∎

Definition 5.3
.

On domains where
{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}\neq 1
we introduce the objects

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{S}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{abcd}={}

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{W}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{abcd}-\frac{6({{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{ab}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{cd}-\tfrac{1}{3}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}{{\mathcal{I}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{abcd})}{1-{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}},

(5.8a)

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{S}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}={}

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{S}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{abcd}{{\mathcal{S}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{abcd},

(5.8b)

\displaystyle{{\mathbf{s}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}={}

\displaystyle\frac{{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}}{(1-{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}})^{4}},

(5.8c)

respectively. Here
{{\mathcal{I}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{abcd}=\tfrac{1}{4}(g_{ac}g_{bd}-g_{ad}g_{bc}\pm\epsilon_{abcd})
is the metric on SD and ASD 2-forms.

5.1.
Characterizations of special geometry

The following result holds independently for the SD and ASD sides. Recall the Petrov classification of Weyl tensors on Riemannian 4-manifolds
[
35
]
, see also
[
49
,
1
]
.

Lemma 5.4
.

{{\mathcal{W}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{abcd}
is of Petrov type
{{D}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}
if one of the following equivalent conditions hold.

(1)

{{\mathcal{S}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{abcd}=0
and
{{\mathcal{W}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{abcd}\neq 0
.

(2)

{{\mathbf{s}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}=\text{const.}\neq 0
.

Proof.

We first show the equivalence of the conditions. From (
5.13
) and
{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}\neq 0
we have (1)
\Rightarrow
(2). From (
5.15
) we have (2)
\Rightarrow{{\mathcal{S}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}=0
which implies (1).

Suppose condition (1) holds. Then we have

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{W}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{abcd}=\frac{6({{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{ab}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{cd}-\tfrac{1}{3}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}{{\mathcal{I}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427 0,

(5.9)

which implies that
{{\mathcal{W}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{abcd}
has degenerate eigenvalues and hence is of Petrov type
{{D}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}
.
∎

(5.15)

Now (

) follows by expanding in

Lemma 5.8

(Divergence Identity)
.

Let
J_{T}{}^{a},J_{D}{}^{a},J_{E}{}^{a}
be as in (
4.15

with equality only if
