is algebraically special.

Proof.

Equation (
5.26
) is the case
\beta=1
of (
5.22
). By Lemma
5.1
, we have that
-1<{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}<1
. Further, by Lemma
5.4
and Proposition
5.7
with
\beta=1
,
{{V}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}\geq 0
, with equality only if
{{\mathcal{W}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{abcd}
is algebraically special.
∎

6.
Integrating the divergence identity

In this section we shall investigate the consequences of the divergence identity (
5.26
) presented in Lemma
5.10
. By Lemma
5.1
,
-1<{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}<1
and hence

{{\Psi}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{a}
is smooth for
\lambda>0
and
{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}>0
, while it is singular on the set

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}=\{{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}=0\}=\{{{\mathbf{s}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}=0\}

(6.1)

and on the fixed point set
F=\{\lambda=0\}
of the
S^{1}
-action. Integrating (
5.26
) over a bounded domain
\Omega\subset\mathcal{M}\setminus({{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}\cup F)

yields

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\nabla^{a}{{\Psi}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{a}\,d\mu=\int_{\partial\Omega}n^{a}{{\Psi}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{a}\,d\mu

(6.2)

where
n^{a}
is the outward pointing normal to
\partial\Omega
. We then consider a family
\Omega_{\epsilon}
exhausting
\mathcal{M}({{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}\cup F)
and evaluate the limiting boundary contributions in terms of fixed point data. Using the
G
-signature formula, this is then used to give conditions under which
(\mathcal{M},g_{ab})
must be algebraically special, which in turn yields uniqueness results.

Remark 6.1
.

In this paper we shall consider applications of the identity (
5.22
) for
\beta=1
. It is only in this case that the boundary terms at the fixed point set are local, in the sense that they do not depend on the pointwise values of globally defined quantities such as
{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}
, see Lemma
6.8
.

Lemma 6.2
.

Near a nut
P
with surface gravities
\kappa^{1},\kappa^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}={{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}|_{P}+O(\lambda)=2|\kappa^{1}\pm\kappa^{2}|+O(\lambda).

(6.3)

Near a bolt
B
with surface gravity
\kappa
we have

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}={{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}|_{B}+O(\lambda)=2|\kappa|+O(\lambda).

(6.4)

Proof.

From (
4.6d
) we have
\nabla_{c}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}=0
at points where
\xi=0
. So
{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}={{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{P}+O(\lambda)
resp.
{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}={{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}|_{B}+O(\lambda)
. Further,
{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}|_{B}
is constant along a bolt. A computation using (
3.1
) and (
3.4
) gives us
{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}|_{P}=2|\kappa^{1}\pm\kappa^{2}|
and
{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}|_{B}=2|\kappa|
.
∎

Proposition 6.3
.

Let
{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}
be the singular set given in (
6.1
) and let
F=\{\lambda=0\}
be the fixed point set of the
S^{1}
action. Assume that
(\mathcal{M},g_{ab})
is not half-flat. Then

(1)

{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}
is compact,

(2)

{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}
and
F
are disjoint.

Proof.

Since
{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}
is the zero set of a continuous function, it is closed. By (
4.6b
) and Lemma
5.1
we have

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}=\lambda^{-1}{{\sigma}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{a}{{\sigma}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{a}=\lambda^{-1}(\nabla_{a}{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}})(\nabla^{a}{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}})={{b}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}r^{-4}+O^{*}(r^{-4-\delta})

(6.5)

and thus
r^{4}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}\to{{b}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}\neq 0
at infinity. Hence
{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}
is bounded and therefore compact.

Suppose
\lambda={{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}=0
at a point
p
. From (
4.6d
) it follows that the derivative of
{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}
vanishes at
p
. Taking further derivatives of (
4.6d
) and using (
4.2b
), (
4.2c
) and (
4.6c
) we see that every term in every derivative of
{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}
contains either a factor
\xi_{a}
or a factor
{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{bd}
. It follows that all derivatives of
{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}
vanish at
p
. Since
(\mathcal{M},g_{ab})
is Ricci-flat we may work in coordinates where all geometric quantities are analytic. It follows that
{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}=0
everywhere, which by Proposition
5.2
contradicts the assumption that
(\mathcal{M},g_{ab})
is not half-flat.
∎

Proposition 6.4
.

The set
{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}
is countably 2-rectifiable, and therefore has Hausdorff dimension at most
2
.

Remark 6.5
.

The fact that
{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}
is 2-rectifiable means that it can be written as a countable union of sets of the form
\Phi(\Xi)
, where
\Xi\subset\mathbb{R}^{2}
is bounded and
\Phi:\Xi\to\mathcal{M}
is a Lipschitz map. In particular, the Hausdorff dimension of
{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}
is at most 2.

Proof.

Taking the total anti-symmetrization respectively the trace of (
4.6c
) and using the first Bianchi identity respectively the fact that
{{\mathcal{W}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{abcd}
is trace-free, we find that the 2-form
{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{ab}
is closed and coclosed. From
[
5
, Main Theorem]
we thus know that
{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}
, which is the zero set of
{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{ab}
, is countably 2-rectifiable.
∎

Fix small
\delta,\epsilon>0
. Since the Hausdorff dimension of
{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}
is at most 2 there is a finite cover of
{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}
by balls
B_{p_{i}}(r_{i})
of radius
r_{i}
such that
\sum_{i}r_{i}^{2+\delta}<\epsilon
. Define

\displaystyle U_{{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.87502pt}{\resizebox{485301}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83336pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87502pt}{\resizebox{485301}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83336pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{489616}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{563125}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.20833pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}},\epsilon}=\cup_{i}B_{p_{i}}(r_{i}).

(6.6)

For
\epsilon>0
define

\displaystyle U_{\infty,\epsilon}=\{r>\epsilon^{-1}\},\quad U_{F,\epsilon}=\{\lambda<\epsilon\}.

(6.7)

If
\epsilon
is sufficiently small, the sets
U_{{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.87502pt}{\resizebox{485301}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83336pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87502pt}{\resizebox{485301}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83336pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{489616}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{563125}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.20833pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}},\epsilon}
,
U_{F,\epsilon}
,
U_{{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.87502pt}{\resizebox{485301}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83336pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87502pt}{\resizebox{485301}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83336pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{489616}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{563125}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.20833pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}},\epsilon}
are all disjoint. Further, define

\displaystyle{{\Omega}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{\epsilon}=\mathcal{M}\setminus(U_{\infty,\epsilon}\cup U_{F,\epsilon}\cup U_{{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.87502pt}{\resizebox{485301}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83336pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87502pt}{\resizebox{485301}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83336pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{489616}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{563125}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.20833pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}},\epsilon}).

(6.8)

The sets
\mathcal{M}\setminus U_{\infty,\epsilon}
give an exhaustion of
\mathcal{M}
as
\epsilon\to 0
with the domains
U_{\infty,\epsilon}
receding to infinity along the end. The domains
U_{F,\epsilon}
surround the fixed point set
F
, and for
\epsilon\to 0
converge to the nuts and bolts. Finally, the domains
U_{{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.87502pt}{\resizebox{485301}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83336pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87502pt}{\resizebox{485301}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83336pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{489616}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{563125}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.20833pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}},\epsilon}
surround the set
{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}
. It follows from the just mentioned facts that the family of domains
{{\Omega}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{\epsilon}
forms an exhaustion of
\mathcal{M}\setminus(F\cup{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}})
. We have

\displaystyle\partial{{\Omega}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{\epsilon}=\partial U_{\infty,\epsilon}\cup\partial U_{F,\epsilon}\cup\partial U_{{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.87502pt}{\resizebox{485301}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83336pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87502pt}{\resizebox{485301}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83336pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{489616}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{563125}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.20833pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}},\epsilon}.

(6.9)

By construction,
{{\Psi}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{a}
is smooth in
{{\Omega}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{\epsilon}
. Therefore, in order to evaluate the right-hand side of (
6.2
), we may consider fluxes of
{{\Psi}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{a}
through each of these boundary components in (
6.9
), and take the limit
\epsilon\to 0
. The boundaries
\partial U_{\infty,\epsilon}
and
\partial U_{F,\epsilon}
are smooth hypersurfaces for small
\epsilon
, while the boundary
\partial U_{{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.87502pt}{\resizebox{485301}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83336pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87502pt}{\resizebox{485301}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83336pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{489616}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{563125}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.20833pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}},\epsilon}
is smooth almost everywhere. The flux through
\partial U_{\infty,\epsilon}
gives the contribution from infinity, while that through
\partial U_{F,\epsilon}
gives the contribution from the nuts and bolts. Finally, the flux through
\partial U_{{{\mathcal{Z}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.87502pt}{\resizebox{485301}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83336pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.87502pt}{\resizebox{485301}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83336pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{489616}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{563125}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.20833pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}},\epsilon}
yields the contribution from the set where
{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}=0
.

Definition 6.6

(Length at infinity)
.

For
p\in M
, let
\ell(p)
denote the length of the
S^{1}
orbit through
p
. The
length at infinity
of
(\mathcal{M},g_{ab})
is defined as

\displaystyle\ell_{\infty}=\liminf_{p\to\infty}\ell(p).

(6.10)

The remainder of this section is devoted to the proof of the following result.

Proposition 6.7

(Boundary terms)
.

Let
(\mathcal{M},g_{ab})
be an ALF
S^{1}
-instanton that is not half-flat. Let
O
be the base of the boundary at infinity of
(\mathcal{M},g_{ab})
and let
\chi[O]
be its orbifold Euler characteristic, see Remark
2.12
. Then

\displaystyle-2\pi\ell_{\infty}\chi[O]+4\pi^{2}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n_{\text{\rm bolts}}}\frac{\chi[B_{i}]}{|\kappa(B_{i})|}\pm\sum_{i=1}^{n_{\text{\rm nuts}}}\epsilon(P_{i})\frac{|\kappa^{1}(P_{i})\pm\kappa^{2}(P_{i})|}{|\kappa^{1}(P_{i})\kappa^{2}(P_{i})|}\right)\geq 0

(6.11)

with equality if and only if
\mathcal{M}
is algebraically special.

Lemma 6.8
.

Let
(\mathcal{M},g_{ab})
be an ALF
S^{1}
-instanton that is not half-flat. Let
{{\Psi}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{a}
be given by (
5.25
). Then there is a locally bounded one-form
\Xi_{a}
such that

\displaystyle{{\Psi}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{a}=\pm\frac{{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}}{2}J^{T}_{a}+\frac{\nabla_{a}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}}{2\lambda}+\Xi_{a}.

(6.12)

Proof.

We note

\displaystyle\frac{1+{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mp$}}}}}}}}{1-{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}}=1+\frac{{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}+{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mp$}}}}}}}}{1-{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}}=1+\frac{2\lambda}{1-{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}},

(6.13)

and with
{{J}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{a}
given by (
5.20
) we have

\displaystyle{{J}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{a}\cdot\frac{1}{(1-{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}})^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\pm(1\mp{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.76923pt}{\resizebox{481531}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle+$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.76923pt}{\resizebox{481531}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle+$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.57141pt}{\resizebox{516533}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle+$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.57141pt}{\resizebox{583338}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}}}}})^{2}{{\sigma}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{392747}{262143}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle-$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{392747}{262143}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle-$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{429219}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle-$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{519722}{262143}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}}}}}}_{a}\mp(1\pm{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{392747}{262143}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\displaystyle-$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{392747}{262143}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\textstyle-$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{429219}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptstyle-$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{519722}{262143}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}}}}}})^{2}{{\sigma}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.76923pt}{\resizebox{481531}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle+$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.76923pt}{\resizebox{481531}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle+$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.57141pt}{\resizebox{516533}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle+$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.57141pt}{\resizebox{583338}{262144}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle+$}}}}}}}_{a}}{2\lambda^{2}}\cdot\frac{1}{(1-{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\ (6.15)

The result follows.
∎

6.1.
Contribution from the fixed point set

Lemma 6.9

(Boundary term at the nuts)
.

Let
P
be a nut with surface gravities
\kappa^{1},\kappa^{2}
. Let
U_{P,\epsilon}
be the connected neighbourhood of
P
where
\lambda<\epsilon
and let
S_{P,\epsilon}=\partial U_{P,\epsilon}
be its boundary with inward pointing unit normal
n^{a}
. Then

\displaystyle\lim_{\epsilon\to 0}\int_{S_{P,\epsilon}}n^{a}{{\Psi}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{a}\,d\mu=\pm 4\pi^{2}\frac{|\kappa^{1}\pm\kappa^{2}|}{\kappa^{1}\kappa^{2}}.

(6.16)

Proof.

By Lemma
6.2
, Proposition
4.5
, and Lemma
6.8
we have

\displaystyle\int_{S_{P,\epsilon}}n^{a}{{\Psi}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{a}\,d\mu

\displaystyle=\int_{S_{P,\epsilon}}n^{a}\left(\pm\frac{{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}}{2}J^{T}_{a}+\frac{\nabla_{a}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}}{2\lambda}+\Xi_{a}\right)\,d\mu

\displaystyle=\int_{S_{P,\epsilon}}n^{a}\left(\pm\frac{2|\kappa^{1}\pm\kappa^{2}|+O(\lambda)}{2}J^{T}_{a}+O(\lambda^{-1})\right)\,d\mu

\displaystyle=\pm|\kappa^{1}\pm\kappa^{2}|\cdot 8\pi N(P)+O(\epsilon)+\int_{S_{P,\epsilon}}O(\lambda^{-1})\,d\mu

\displaystyle=\pm 4\pi^{2}\frac{|\kappa^{1}\pm\kappa^{2}|}{\kappa^{1}\kappa^{2}}+O(\epsilon^{1/2}),

(6.17)

and the result follows.
∎

Lemma 6.10

(Boundary term at the bolts)
.

Let
B
be a bolt with surface gravity
\kappa
. Let
U_{B,\epsilon}
be the connected neighbourhood of
B
where
\lambda<\epsilon
and let
S_{B,\epsilon}=\partial U_{B,\epsilon}
be its boundary with inward pointing unit normal
n^{a}
. Then

\displaystyle\lim_{\epsilon\to 0}\int_{S_{B,\epsilon}}n^{a}{{\Psi}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{a}\,d\mu=\frac{4\pi^{2}}{|\kappa|}\chi[B].

(6.18)

Proof.

By Lemma
6.2
, Proposition
4.5
, and Lemma
6.8
we have

\displaystyle\int_{S_{B,\epsilon}}n^{a}{{\Psi}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{a}\,d\mu

\displaystyle=\int_{S_{B,\epsilon}}n^{a}\left(\pm\frac{{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}}{2}J^{T}_{a}+\frac{\nabla_{a}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}}{2\lambda}+\Xi_{a}\right)\,d\mu

\displaystyle=\pm\frac{2|\kappa|+O(\epsilon)}{2}8\pi N(B)+O(\epsilon^{1/2})

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1+O(\epsilon)}{2\epsilon}\int_{S_{B,\epsilon}}\frac{1}{2{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}}n^{a}\nabla_{a}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}\,d\mu

\displaystyle=\mp\left(|\kappa|+O(\epsilon)\right)\cdot\frac{8\pi}{8\kappa^{2}}\int_{B}\mathcal{R}^{\parallel\perp}d\mu+O(\epsilon^{1/2})

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1+O(\epsilon)}{4\epsilon(2|\kappa|+O(\epsilon))}\int_{S_{B,\epsilon}}n^{a}\nabla_{a}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}\,d\mu.

(6.19)

We use (
4.19
) and (
4.17
) to compute on
B
,

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{W}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{abcd}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{cd}

\displaystyle=(W_{abcd}\pm\frac{1}{2}\epsilon_{cd}{}^{ef}W_{abef})\kappa(\epsilon^{\perp}{}^{cd}\pm\epsilon^{\parallel}{}^{cd})

\displaystyle=\kappa(R_{abcd}\pm\frac{1}{2}R_{abef}\epsilon_{cd}{}^{ef})(\epsilon^{\perp}{}^{cd}\pm\epsilon^{\parallel}{}^{cd})

\displaystyle=\kappa(R_{abcd}\epsilon^{\perp}{}^{cd}\pm\frac{1}{2}R_{abef}\epsilon_{cd}{}^{ef}\epsilon^{\perp}{}^{cd})

\displaystyle\qquad\pm\kappa(R_{abcd}\epsilon^{\parallel}{}^{cd}\pm\frac{1}{2}R_{abef}\epsilon_{cd}{}^{ef}\epsilon^{\parallel}{}^{cd})

\displaystyle=\kappa(R_{abcd}\epsilon^{\perp}{}^{cd}\pm R_{abef}\epsilon^{\parallel}{}^{ef})

\displaystyle\qquad\pm\kappa(R_{abcd}\epsilon^{\parallel}{}^{cd}\pm R_{abef}\epsilon^{\perp}{}^{ef})

\displaystyle=2\kappa(R_{abcd}\epsilon^{\perp}{}^{cd}\pm R_{abcd}\epsilon^{\parallel}{}^{cd}),

(6.20)

and with (
4.23
) and (
4.24
) we get

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{W}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{abcd}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{ab}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{cd}

\displaystyle=2\kappa^{2}(R_{abcd}\epsilon^{\perp}{}^{cd}\pm R_{abcd}\epsilon^{\parallel}{}^{cd})(\epsilon^{\perp}{}^{ab}\pm\epsilon^{\parallel}{}^{ab})

\displaystyle=2\kappa^{2}(R_{abcd}\epsilon^{\perp}{}^{ab}\epsilon^{\perp}{}^{cd}\pm R_{abcd}\epsilon^{\perp}{}^{ab}\epsilon^{\parallel}{}^{cd})

\displaystyle\qquad\pm 2\kappa^{2}(R_{abcd}\epsilon^{\parallel}{}^{ab}\epsilon^{\perp}{}^{cd}\pm R_{abcd}\epsilon^{\parallel}{}^{ab}\epsilon^{\parallel}{}^{cd})

\displaystyle=2\kappa^{2}(2\mathcal{R}^{\perp\perp}\pm 2\mathcal{R}^{\perp\parallel})\pm 2\kappa^{2}(2\mathcal{R}^{\perp\parallel}\pm 2\mathcal{R}^{\parallel\parallel})

\displaystyle=4\kappa^{2}(\mathcal{R}^{\perp\perp}+\mathcal{R}^{\parallel\parallel})\pm 8\kappa^{2}\mathcal{R}^{\perp\parallel}

\displaystyle=8\kappa^{2}(R^{B}\pm\mathcal{R}^{\perp\parallel}).

(6.21)

Taking into account the fact that
n^{a}
is the inward pointing unit normal to
S_{B,\epsilon}
with respect to
U_{B,\epsilon}
, we have using
(
4.6g
),

\displaystyle\int_{S_{B,\epsilon}}n^{a}\nabla_{a}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}\,d\mu

\displaystyle=-\int_{U_{B,\epsilon}}\nabla^{a}\nabla_{a}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}\,d\mu

\displaystyle=-\int_{U_{B,\epsilon}}\left(-{{\mathcal{W}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{abcd}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{ab}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{cd}+\frac{1}{2}\lambda{{\mathcal{W}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}\right)\,d\mu

\displaystyle=\int_{U_{B,\epsilon}}{{\mathcal{W}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{abcd}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{ab}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{cd}\,d\mu-\frac{\epsilon}{2}\int_{U_{B,\epsilon}}{{\mathcal{W}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}^{2}\,d\mu

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi\epsilon}{\kappa^{2}}\int_{B}8\kappa^{2}(R^{B}\pm\mathcal{R}^{\perp\parallel})\,d\mu+\frac{\epsilon}{2}O(\epsilon^{1/2}).

(6.22)

In the last step here, the integral over the tube
U_{B,\epsilon}
is approximated with the integral over
B
multiplied with the area
\frac{\pi\epsilon}{\kappa^{2}}
of the normal disk. Together we find

\displaystyle\int_{S_{B,\epsilon}}n^{a}{{\Psi}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{a}\,d\mu

\displaystyle=\mp\pi\frac{|\kappa|+O(\epsilon)}{\kappa^{2}}\int_{B}\mathcal{R}^{\parallel\perp}\,d\mu+O(\epsilon^{1/2})

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1+O(\epsilon)}{4\epsilon(2|\kappa|+O(\epsilon))}\left(\frac{\pi\epsilon}{\kappa^{2}}\int_{B}8\kappa^{2}(R^{B}\pm\mathcal{R}^{\perp\parallel})\,d\mu+\frac{\epsilon}{2}O(\epsilon^{1/2})\right)

\displaystyle=\mp\pi\frac{|\kappa|+O(\epsilon)}{\kappa^{2}}\int_{B}\mathcal{R}^{\parallel\perp}\,d\mu+O(\epsilon^{1/2})

\displaystyle\qquad+\pi\frac{1+O(\epsilon)}{|\kappa|+O(\epsilon)}\left(\int_{B}(R^{B}\pm\mathcal{R}^{\perp\parallel})\,d\mu+\frac{\epsilon}{2}O(\epsilon^{1/2})\right).

(6.23)

Finally, the Gauss–Bonnet theorem tells us that

\displaystyle\lim_{\epsilon\to 0}\int_{S_{B,\epsilon}}n^{a}{{\Psi}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{a}\,d\mu

\displaystyle=\mp\frac{\pi}{|\kappa|}\int_{B}\mathcal{R}^{\parallel\perp}\,d\mu+\frac{\pi}{|\kappa|}\int_{B}(R^{B}\pm\mathcal{R}^{\parallel\perp})\,d\mu

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi}{|\kappa|}\int_{B}R^{B}\,d\mu

\displaystyle=\frac{2\pi}{|\kappa|}\int_{B}\mathcal{K}^{B}\,d\mu

\displaystyle=\frac{4\pi^{2}}{|\kappa|}\chi[B].

(6.24)

∎

6.2.
Contribution from infinity

Lemma 6.11

(Length at infinity)
.

Let
(M,g)
be an ALF-
A_{k}

S^{1}
-instanton, with
S^{1}
action generated by
\xi
, with
\lambda\to 1
at
\infty
.
Let
\Pi
be the minimum period of the linearized
S^{1}
action at the fixed point set,

\displaystyle\Pi=\min\left\{\frac{2\pi}{|\kappa(P)|},\frac{2\pi}{|\kappa(B)|}\right\},

(6.25)

where the minimum is taken over all nuts and bolts in the fixed point set
F
, and all surface gravities.
The length at infinity
\ell_{\infty}
, see Definition
6.6
, satisfies

\displaystyle\ell_{\infty}\geq\Pi.

(6.26)

Proof.

Assume for a contradiction there is an
\epsilon>0
such that for any
r_{0}
sufficiently large there is
p\in M
,
r(p)>r_{0}
with with
\ell(p)<(1-\epsilon)\Pi
. We may without loss of generality assume
\lambda(p)>1-\epsilon
for
r(p)>r_{0}
, by choosing
r_{0}
large enough.

Since
\lambda\to 1
at
\infty
,
F
is compact. Hence, there is a minimizing geodesic
\gamma
connecting
p
to
F
. Let
\gamma(0)\in F
be the starting point of
\gamma
. The period of the
S^{1}
action on
\gamma
is determined by the period of the linearized action at
x
on the initial velocity vector
\dot{\gamma}(0)
. Let the period of the
S^{1}
action on
\gamma
be
\Pi_{\gamma}
. In view of (
6.25
),
\Pi_{\gamma}\geq\Pi
. We have that

\displaystyle(1-\epsilon)\Pi>\ell(p)=\lambda^{1/2}(p)\Pi_{\gamma}>(1-\epsilon/2)\Pi

(6.27)

which is a contradiction. This proves the lemma.
∎

Lemma 6.12

(The boundary term at infinity)
.

Let
S_{\infty,\epsilon}=\partial U_{\infty,\epsilon}=\{r=\epsilon^{-1}\}
with outward outward-pointing unit normal
n^{a}
. Then

\displaystyle\lim_{\epsilon\to 0}\int_{S_{\infty,\epsilon}}n^{a}{{\Psi}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{a}\,d\mu\leq-2\pi\ell_{\infty}\chi[O].

(6.28)

Proof.

By (
5.2
) we have

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}

\displaystyle=1+{{b}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}r^{-1}+O^{*}(r^{-1-\delta}),

\displaystyle{{\sigma}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{a}=\nabla_{a}{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}

\displaystyle=-{{b}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}r^{-2}\nabla_{a}r+O^{*}(r^{-2-\delta}),

(6.29)

as
r\to\infty
. By (
6.5
) we further have

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}

\displaystyle=|{{b}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}|r^{-2}+O^{*}(r^{-2-\delta})=-{{b}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}r^{-2}+O^{*}(r^{-2-\delta}),

\displaystyle\nabla_{a}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}

\displaystyle=2{{b}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}r^{-3}\nabla_{a}r+O^{*}(r^{-3-\delta}).

(6.30)

We find that

\displaystyle{{\Psi}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{a}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}{{J}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{a}\frac{{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}}{(1-{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}})^{2}}+\frac{(1+{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mp$}}}}}}})(1-{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}})}{2\lambda}\nabla_{a}\left(\frac{{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}}{(1-{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}})^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4\lambda^{2}}\left({{\sigma}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mp$}}}}}}}_{a}-\frac{(1+{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mp$}}}}}}})^{2}}{(1-{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}})^{2}}{{\sigma}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}_{a}\right){{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1+{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mp$}}}}}}}}{2\lambda(1-{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}})}\nabla_{a}{{\mathcal{F}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\pm$}}}}}}}+\frac{1+{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\textstyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{451967}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.40833pt}{$\scriptstyle\mp$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.49998pt}{\resizebox{510420}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.29166pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mp$}}}}}}}}{\lambda(1-{{\mathcal{E}}^{\hskip-0.5pt\mathchoice{\raisebox{-0.67308pt}{\resizebox{427220}{229376}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.83334pt}{$\displaystyle\pm$}}}}}{\raisebox{-0.67308
